    SELECT TOP(1) [p].[Id], [p].[ClienteId], [p].[Codigo], [p].[DataHora], [p].[DataImpressao], [p].[Delivery], [p].[FormaPagamento], [p].[PagamentoId], [p].[Status], [p].[StatusImpressao], [p].[Subtotal], [p].[TentativasImpressao], [p].[TipoEntrega], [p].[Total], [p].[TrocoPara], [c].[Id] AS [Id0], [c].[Nome], [c].[Observacao], [c].[Telefone], [c].[Endereco_Bairro], [c].[Endereco_Cidade], [c].[Endereco_Complemento], [c].[Endereco_Estado], [c].[Endereco_Numero], [c].[Endereco_Rua]
    FROM [Pedidos] AS [p]
    INNER JOIN [Clientes] AS [c] ON [p].[ClienteId] = [c].[Id]
    WHERE [p].[Id] = @__id_0
) AS [t]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [i].[Id], [i].[Observacao], [i].[PedidoId], [i].[ProdutoId], [i].[Quantidade], [i].[Valor], [i].[ValorUnitario], [p0].[Id] AS [Id0], [p0].[Ativo], [p0].[CategoriaId], [p0].[Descricao], [p0].[Nome], [p0].[Preco], [t1].[Id] AS [Id1], [t1].[AdicionalId], [t1].[ItemPedidoId], [t1].[Preco] AS [Preco0], [t1].[Id0] AS [Id00], [t1].[Nome] AS [Nome0], [t1].[Preco0] AS [Preco00]
    FROM [ItensPedido] AS [i]
    INNER JOIN [Produtos] AS [p0] ON [i].[ProdutoId] = [p0].[Id]
    LEFT JOIN (
        SELECT [i0].[Id], [i0].[AdicionalId], [i0].[ItemPedidoId], [i0].[Preco], [a].[Id] AS [Id0], [a].[Nome], [a].[Preco] AS [Preco0]
        FROM [ItensPedidoAdicionais] AS [i0]
        INNER JOIN [Adicionais] AS [a] ON [i0].[AdicionalId] = [a].[Id]
    ) AS [t1] ON [i].[Id] = [t1].[ItemPedidoId]
) AS [t0] ON [t].[Id] = [t0].[PedidoId]
ORDER BY [t].[Id], [t].[Id0], [t0].[Id], [t0].[Id0], [t0].[Id1]

    SELECT TOP(1) [p].[Id], [p].[ClienteId], [p].[Codigo], [p].[DataHora], [p].[DataImpressao], [p].[Delivery], [p].[FormaPagamento], [p].[PagamentoId], [p].[Status], [p].[StatusImpressao], [p].[Subtotal], [p].[TentativasImpressao], [p].[TipoEntrega], [p].[Total], [p].[TrocoPara], [c].[Id] AS [Id0], [c].[Nome], [c].[Observacao], [c].[Telefone], [c].[Endereco_Bairro], [c].[Endereco_Cidade], [c].[Endereco_Complemento], [c].[Endereco_Estado], [c].[Endereco_Numero], [c].[Endereco_Rua]
    FROM [Pedidos] AS [p]
    INNER JOIN [Clientes] AS [c] ON [p].[ClienteId] = [c].[Id]
    WHERE [p].[Id] = @__id_0
) AS [t]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [i].[Id], [i].[Observacao], [i].[PedidoId], [i].[ProdutoId], [i].[Quantidade], [i].[Valor], [i].[ValorUnitario], [p0].[Id] AS [Id0], [p0].[Ativo], [p0].[CategoriaId], [p0].[Descricao], [p0].[Nome], [p0].[Preco], [t1].[Id] AS [Id1], [t1].[AdicionalId], [t1].[ItemPedidoId], [t1].[Preco] AS [Preco0], [t1].[Id0] AS [Id00], [t1].[Nome] AS [Nome0], [t1].[Preco0] AS [Preco00]
    FROM [ItensPedido] AS [i]
    INNER JOIN [Produtos] AS [p0] ON [i].[ProdutoId] = [p0].[Id]
    LEFT JOIN (
        SELECT [i0].[Id], [i0].[AdicionalId], [i0].[ItemPedidoId], [i0].[Preco], [a].[Id] AS [Id0], [a].[Nome], [a].[Preco] AS [Preco0]
        FROM [ItensPedidoAdicionais] AS [i0]
        INNER JOIN [Adicionais] AS [a] ON [i0].[AdicionalId] = [a].[Id]
    ) AS [t1] ON [i].[Id] = [t1].[ItemPedidoId]
) AS [t0] ON [t].[Id] = [t0].[PedidoId]
ORDER BY [t].[Id], [t].[Id0], [t0].[Id], [t0].[Id0], [t0].[Id1]
